TL;DR: 本文探讨印度《2019年消费者保护法》是否足以应对AI产品和服务造成的损害，以及是否能在AI价值链中合理分配责任。研究发现该法的定义宽泛且技术中立，可能适用于AI相关伤害，但在因果关系证明、责任主体划分（数据提供者、模型开发者、部署者、用户等角色重叠）以及 proportionate 责任分配机制上存在显著空白。该法虽可能涵盖AI实体，但执行需要澄清行业特定重叠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在消费领域广泛应用，AI相关伤害的赔偿责任问题尚未解决。本文旨在评估印度消费者保护法能否有效应对有缺陷AI产品/服务造成的损害，以及能否在AI价值链中按比例分配责任。

Method: 采用法律分析/工作论文形式，考察《2019年消费者保护法》中产品责任、伤害、缺陷等定义的适用性，分析AI价值链中不同主体（数据提供者、模型开发者、部署者、用户）的责任重叠问题，并探讨因果关系证明等技术性挑战。

Result: 该法的定义宽泛且技术中立，理论上可覆盖AI相关伤害（人身伤害、心理伤害、偏见输出、失控等）。但存在重大空白：（1）AI缺陷与消费者伤害之间的因果关系难以证明（AI故障通常源于设计选择而非离散缺陷）；（2）AI价值链中制造商、销售者、服务提供者等传统角色分类无法映射数据提供者、模型开发者、部署者、用户等重叠责任主体；（3）缺乏针对多利益相关方复杂伤害的比例责任分配机制。

Conclusion: 该法可能涵盖AI实体，但执行层面需澄清行业特定重叠问题。现行责任框架缺乏针对AI复杂、多利益相关方伤害的有效比例分配机制，需要进一步明确和修订。

Abstract: As AI systems proliferate in consumer facing applications, questions about liability for AI related harms remain unresolved. This working paper examines whether India's Consumer Protection Act, 2019, adequately addresses harm caused by defective AI products and services, and whether it proportionately allocates liability across the AI value chain.
  The Act's broad definitions of product liability, harm, and deficiency appear technology agnostic and potentially applicable to AI related incidents including personal injury, psychological harm, biased outputs, and loss of control. However, significant gaps remain. Proving causation between AI defects and consumer harm presents a technical challenge, as AI failures often stem from design choices rather than discrete defects. Additionally, the Act's framework assumes distinct roles for manufacturers, sellers, and service providers, yet the AI value chain involves overlapping responsibilities among data providers, model developers, deployers, and users that do not neatly map to these categories. Current liability frameworks lack proportionate mechanisms to effectively address complex, multistakeholder AI harms. While the Act may cover AI entities, enforcement requires clarification on sector specific overlaps.

</details>


### [203] [ReflectFact: Self-Reflective Agents for Improving Comprehension and Reasoning in Multi-Hop Fact Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12877)
*Runze Zhao,Zixin Tang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Leyuan Chang,Xiaopeng Fu,Boyu Qiao,Dongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReflectFact是一个自反思智能体框架，用于多跳事实验证。它通过显式推理路径规划、证据漂移验证和推理反思验证三个关键任务，解决现有方法中智能体缺乏全局目标意识以及参数知识与证据冲突的问题。在HOVER和EX-FEVER数据集上达到最优性能，分别超越最强基线3.32%和2.78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多智能体协作的多跳事实验证方法存在两个关键限制：(1) 智能体可能在执行子任务时缺乏对全局验证目标的充分意识，导致推理偏离预期方向；(2) 参数知识与所提供的证据之间的冲突可能削弱基于证据的推理，导致错误判决。

Method: ReflectFact框架引入三个关键任务：(1) 显式推理路径规划：通过解析隐含实体、将声明分解为子问题、整合已验证事实到判决中，构建基于证据的推理路径；(2) 证据漂移验证：当基于证据的答案仅仅重复参数先验时，让智能体通过引用支持证据重新回答，校准证据偏差以确保基于证据的理解；(3) 推理反思验证：重新检查每个推理步骤，一旦检测到不一致就重新生成，通过全局任务视角纠正推理缺陷（如位置偏差和替换偏差）。最后，智能体聚合经过验证的推理链以产生可靠判决。

Result: 在HOVER和EX-FEVER数据集上的大量实验表明，ReflectFact有效弥补了现有方法的理解和推理缺陷，实现了最先进的性能，分别比最强基线高出3.32%和2.78%。

Conclusion: ReflectFact通过自反思机制有效解决了多跳事实验证中智能体缺乏全局目标意识和参数知识与证据冲突的问题，为基于证据的推理提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Multi-hop fact verification, which verifies claims by reasoning over multiple pieces of evidence, is critical for combating misinformation on social media yet remains highly challenging. Recent methods primarily rely on multi-agent collaboration to decompose fact verification into specialized subtasks. However, these methods face two critical limitations: (1) agents may perform individual subtasks without sufficient awareness of the global verification objective, causing their reasoning to deviate from the intended direction; and (2) conflicts between parametric knowledge and the provided evidence may undermine evidence-grounded reasoning and lead to incorrect verdicts. To address these challenges, we propose ReflectFact, a novel self-reflective agent framework for multi-hop fact verification. ReflectFact introduces three key tasks. Explicit Reasoning Path Planning builds an evidence-grounded reasoning path by resolving implicit entities, decomposing the claim into sub-questions, and integrating the verified facts into a verdict. Evidence-Drift Verification makes the agent re-answer by quoting the supporting evidence when a grounded answer merely echoes its parametric prior, thereby calibrating evidence deviation to ensure grounded comprehension. Reasoning Reflection Verification re-examines each reasoning step and regenerates it once an inconsistency is detected, correcting reasoning flaws such as location bias and replacement bias through a global task perspective. Subsequently, the agent aggregates validated reasoning chains to yield reliable verdicts. Extensive experiments on HOVER and EX-FEVER demonstrate that ReflectFact effectively remedies the comprehension and reasoning defects of existing methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance and respectively outperforming the strongest baseline by 3.32\% and 2.78\% on the two datasets.

</details>


### [204] [Predictive Memory Localization: Forecasting Selective Intervention Paths from Internal Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12892)
*Jinhao Jing,Tian Zeyu,Lucas Qingyang Fang,Zhisheng Chen,Shuang Chen,Yuhao Luo,Qiannian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出预测性记忆定位（PML），将记忆定位转化为可证伪的边际选择性结果预测和风险感知的干预决策方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活导向（Activation steering）能将局部化表征转化为控制方向，但仅靠局部化无法揭示方向是否具有选择性操作区间（selective operating regime）。现有方法缺乏对方向选择性的预测性检验和因果响应评估。

Method: PML将测量的网格干预路径作为记忆定位的预测对象，分离随机校准的目标移动与语义邻近和功能损伤，比较静态定位和监督几何方法，采用强度分离的低剂量因果响应。在3000条记录、9个数据集、14个领域上进行冻结研究，生成30000条记录-方向-层路径和210000条路径-强度评估。使用预测驱动的选择器决定系数或弃权。

Result: 在第7层，基于几何的RFM/AGOP方向达到13.1%目标任意和12.3%干净任意，比随机高3.6和3.4个百分点。在记录、数据集和领域分组分割中，|α|=0.1的响应是|α|∈{0.25,0.5}结果的最强信号。预测驱动选择器提高了效用，减少了语义邻近损伤，避免了密集扫描中的大部分评估。跨三个残差范数匹配的基础模型，学习方向保持选择性路径增益，低剂量响应达到0.801-0.828的记录留出宏AUROC。

Conclusion: PML将记忆定位转化为可证伪的边际选择性结果预测和风险感知的干预决策，证明了通过学习方向可以在低剂量下保持选择性路径增益，实现高效且安全的干预。

Abstract: Activation steering turns localized representations into control directions, but localization alone does not reveal whether a direction has a selective operating regime. We introduce Predictive Memory Localization (PML), which treats the measured-grid intervention path as the predictive object of memory localization. PML separates random-calibrated target movement from semantic-neighbor and capability damage, and compares static localization and supervised geometry with a strength-disjoint low-dose causal response. Our frozen study covers 3,000 records from nine datasets and fourteen domains, yielding 30,000 distinct record-direction-layer paths and 210,000 distinct path-strength evaluations. At layer 7, the geometry-derived RFM/AGOP direction reaches 13.1% target-any and 12.3% clean-any, exceeding random by 3.6 and 3.4 percentage points under a record-paired bootstrap. Across record-, dataset-, and domain-grouped splits, responses at $|α|=0.1$ are the strongest signal for outcomes at disjoint strengths $|α|\in\{0.25,0.5\}$. On held-out records, a predictor-driven selector chooses a coefficient or abstains, improves utility and reduces semantic-neighbor damage relative to a train-tuned fixed-strength policy, and avoids most evaluations in a dense scan. Across three residual-norm-matched base models, learned directions retain selective-path gains and low-dose responses yield 0.801-0.828 record-held-out macro AUROC. PML therefore turns memory localization into a falsifiable forecast of margin-level selective outcomes and a risk-aware intervention decision.

</details>


### [205] [Agent Behavioral Contracts II: Certifying Compositional Reliability Without Assuming Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12895)
*Varun Pratap Bhardwaj,Garima Singh,Arun Pratap Bhardwaj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过大规模预注册实验（18,000次任务）检验了多智能体系统中常见的条件独立性假设，发现共享相同模型的智能体之间故障存在强正相关（phi=0.916），导致可靠性乘积法高估冗余效益。作者证明拟合依赖模型会随数据量增长失去覆盖率，并提出了一个有限样本的线性规划证书方法，通过Bonferroni-Clopper-Pearson框对测量矩进行约束，该方法单调且随矩族丰富而收紧（十矩到十四矩使区间缩小85.7%）。同时给出了一个可选停止条件下保持第一类错误率0.0471的随时有效证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中常用组件可靠性乘积来计算组合可靠性，这依赖于条件独立性假设。该假设虽被常规陈述却极少被检验。论文旨在系统检验这一假设的有效性，并探讨当独立性不成立时如何给出可靠的可靠性界限。

Method: 1) 预注册实验：使用确定性代码（非LLM裁判）对18,000次任务进行评分，测试两智能体交接场景中相同模型与不同模型的共故障情况。2) 统计学分析：计算log OR、phi系数等关联度量，对比不同模型和不同供应商的效果。3) 理论证明：证明拟合依赖模型的bootstrap界会随n增大而失去覆盖真值（识别缺口O(1)，bootstrap削减O(n^{-1/2})）。4) 提出有限样本证书：基于Bonferroni-Clopper-Pearson框的线性规划方法，对测量共执行矩进行约束，并给出随时有效版本。

Result: 1) 相同模型的两智能体在任一失败时共故障率达90.0%（log OR 6.66，phi 0.916）。2) 更换不同模型降低了所有六个对比中的关联；更换不同供应商（模型已不同）则无此效果（零假设被验证）。3) 正相关导致联合故障率高于独立性乘积，使得冗余被高估。4) Bootstrap界随n增大覆盖率下降，而提出的有限样本证书单调且信息高效：将矩从10个扩展到14个使识别区间缩小85.7%，认证下限从0.2455提升至0.4116。5) 随时有效证书在可选停止下保持第一类错误率0.0471。

Conclusion: 条件独立性假设在多智能体系统中不可靠，共享相同模型会导致强正相关并使乘积法可靠性界限过于乐观。拟合依赖模型的方法不可靠，bootstrap界会随数据增多而退化。论文提出的基于线性规划和Bonferroni-Clopper-Pearson框的有限样本证书方法是一种有效的替代方案，它不假设依赖结构，具有单调性和信息效率，并能提供严格的可靠性下界。常见的依赖统计量（如相关系数）受边际分布约束，在不同故障率条件下可能反转条件排序，需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Compositional reliability bounds for multi-agent systems multiply component reliabilities, a step licensed by a conditional-independence assumption that is routinely stated and rarely tested. We test it. Two instances of one model, in a two-agent handoff, co-fail on 90.0% of the missions on which either fails (log OR 6.66, 95% CI [6.38, 7.00]; phi 0.916), in a preregistered evaluation of 18,000 missions scored by deterministic code with no LLM judge. Substituting a different model reduces the association in six of six contrasts; substituting a different vendor, model already different, does not -- a registered hypothesis reported as a null.
  The error is signed and runs against the operator: positive dependence inflates joint failure above the independence product, so redundancy is over-credited exactly when components share a model. The assumption-free alternative is often vacuous, and fitting a dependence model is worse: we prove a bootstrap bound on a fitted model's functional loses coverage of the truth as n grows, the identification gap being O(1) while the bootstrap haircut is O(n^{-1/2}). More data makes such a certificate worse, with no visible symptom.
  We give a finite-sample certificate assuming no dependence structure: a linear program over the joint, over a Bonferroni-Clopper-Pearson box around measured co-execution moments. It is sound, sharp for the information supplied, and monotone in the moment family. Enriching ten moment functionals to fourteen narrows the identified interval by 85.7% and lifts the certified floor from 0.2455 to 0.4116. A companion anytime-valid certificate holds type-I error at 0.0471 under optional stopping.
  Common dependence statistics are marginal-bounded and can reverse an apparent ordering of conditions when the compared agents fail at different rates. Contracts, scoring code, analysis scripts, and the preregistration are released.

</details>


### [206] [Polish Medical Visual Question Answering: Vision-Language Models Underutilize Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12928)
*Jakub Pokrywka,Łukasz Grzybowski,Antoni Lasik,Marek Kubis,Jeremi Ignacy Kaczmarek,Wojciech Kusa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个波兰语医学视觉问答（VQA）基准测试，该基准基于波兰医师和牙医专科认证考试的题目构建，包含多种医学专业和视觉领域的图像题目以及纯文本问答对照集。评估结果显示，最佳模型在完整VQA集上达到79.0%准确率，仅GPT-5.6在可用候选答案的子集上超过人类参考水平，其他所有模型均不如人类。模型从问题文本中获取的信息比图像更多，且在图像主导问题上表现更差，同时仅凭答案选项就能获得高于随机水平的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VQA基准主要集中在英语和其他主流语言上，缺乏针对波兰语等低资源语言的评估。医学专科认证考试题目是评估模型临床知识和视觉理解能力的理想来源，因此作者构建了波兰语医学VQA基准，以填补该语言领域的空白并系统评估现有模型的视觉语言能力。

Method: 从波兰医师和牙医专科认证考试中收集包含图像的题目，构建了覆盖多种医学专科和视觉领域的VQA基准，并设置纯文本QA对照集。评估了波兰语专用、通用开源和商业视觉语言模型。通过比较完整输入与去除图像、去除问题或两者都去除的配置来评估视觉基础能力，并根据图像重要性对题目进行分类。

Result: 最佳模型在完整VQA集上准确率为79.0%；仅有GPT-5.6在可用候选答案的子集上超过人类参考；所有其他模型表现均不如人类。模型从问题文本中获得的信息多于图像，在图像主导问题上表现更差。在QA和VQA任务中，模型仅凭答案选项就能获得高于随机水平的准确率。

Conclusion: 波兰语医学VQA任务仍然具有挑战性，现有模型在视觉推理方面存在局限，尤其是在图像主导问题上。模型过度依赖文本线索和答案选项的统计特征，视觉信息的利用不足。该基准为评估波兰语医学领域的多模态理解能力提供了有价值的资源，并揭示了当前模型的不足。

Abstract: We introduce a Polish-language medical visual question answering (VQA) benchmark, built from Polish Board Certification Examination questions for licensed physicians and dentists pursuing specialist certification. The benchmark comprises image-containing questions spanning diverse medical specialties and visual domains, together with a text-only question answering (QA) control set. We evaluate Polish-oriented, general-purpose open-weight, and commercial vision-language models. The task remains challenging: the best model achieves 79.0\% accuracy on the full VQA set, and only GPT-5.6 surpasses the approximate human reference on the subset with available candidate responses; all other evaluated models perform worse than humans. To assess visual grounding, we compare complete inputs with configurations omitting the image, the question, or both, and categorize questions by image importance. Models derive more useful information from the question text than from the image and perform worse on image-dominant questions. Across both QA and VQA, they nevertheless achieve above-chance accuracy from the answer choices alone, showing that non-trivial performance can persist even when key task components are missing.

</details>


### [207] [FlashDrive: Flash Vision-Language-Action Inference for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12932)
*Zekai Li,Yihao Liang,Hongfei Zhang,Jian Chen,Yesheng Liang,Zhijian Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FlashDrive，一种算法-系统协同设计框架，同时针对视觉编码、语言模型预填充、推理令牌生成和流匹配去噪四个阶段的效率瓶颈，通过流式KV缓存复用、非自回归扩散起草器进行投机解码、自适应步长缓存以及CUDA Graph编译与内核融合，将10B参数的VLA自动驾驶模型端到端延迟从717ms降至151ms（4.7倍），帧率从1.4Hz提升至6.6Hz，精度几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型为端到端自动驾驶带来了推理能力，但其计算开销极高，难以满足实时控制需求。核心挑战在于VLA推理并非单一瓶颈，而是由视觉编码、语言模型预填充、推理令牌生成和流匹配去噪四个阶段级联组成的多重瓶颈，单独优化任何一个阶段都无法解决整体延迟问题，亟需一个同时针对所有阶段的高效协同方案。

Method: FlashDrive采用算法-系统协同设计，针对四个瓶颈分别提出轻量级捷径：1）利用时间重叠在视频帧间实现流式KV缓存复用，消除冗余编码；2）利用驾驶域推理的低熵特性和强块内相关性，设计非自回归扩散起草器用于投机解码，加速串行令牌生成；3）利用速度场两端陡峭、中部平坦的结构，引入自适应步长缓存，在关键区域集中计算资源；4）在系统层叠加CUDA Graph编译和内核融合进一步优化。最终以W4A8量化应用于Alpamayo 1.5-10B模型。

Result: 在Alpamayo 1.5-10B模型上，FlashDrive将端到端延迟从717ms降至151ms（加速4.7倍），帧率从1.4Hz提升至6.6Hz；精度几乎不变：minADE6@6.4s仅偏移0.08m，minADE1指标改善，闭环仿真中碰撞率和偏离道路率均下降。

Conclusion: FlashDrive通过多阶段协同优化，在单GPU上使10B参数推理VLA模型达到6.6Hz的实时性，显著推动了端到端自动驾驶向实际部署迈进，证明了算法-系统协同设计在解决复杂级联瓶颈中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models promise to bring end-to-end reasoning to autonomous driving, but their computational cost remains far too high for real-time control. The core challenge is structural: VLA inference is not a single bottleneck but a cascade of four. Visual encoding wastes compute on overlapping video frames; language-model prefill recomputes context that could be carried over from the previous timestep; reasoning tokens are generated serially despite low entropy; and flow-matching denoising applies uniform compute to a non-uniform velocity field. Addressing any one stage in isolation leaves the others untouched. We propose FlashDrive, an algorithm-system co-design framework that targets all four stages simultaneously. Our key insight is that each bottleneck admits a distinct, lightweight algorithmic shortcut: temporal overlap enables streaming KV-cache reuse across frames; the low per-token entropy and strong intra-block correlations of driving-domain reasoning make a non-autoregressive diffusion drafter highly effective for speculative decoding; and the velocity field's structure---sharp at the endpoints, flat in the middle---permits adaptive step caching that concentrates compute where it matters. Layered on system-level CUDA Graph compilation and kernel fusion, these techniques compound. Applied to Alpamayo 1.5-10B with W4A8 quantization, FlashDrive reduces end-to-end latency from 717ms to 151ms (4.7x) while leaving accuracy essentially unchanged: minADE6@6.4s shifts by only 0.08m, minADE1 improves, and closed-loop collision and off-road rates improve in simulation. By raising a 10B-parameter reasoning VLA from 1.4~Hz to 6.6~Hz on a single GPU, FlashDrive moves end-to-end autonomous driving substantially closer to real-time deployment.

</details>


### [208] [Decomposition of Evidence, Contradiction, and Fragility in Perturbation Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12935)
*Lei You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 DECAF 分解，将扰动解释中的响应幅度拆分为证据（E）、矛盾（C）和脆弱性（F）三个分量，从而区分模型反应的语义含义（支持、反对或无关），而非仅关注数值大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扰动方法仅测量输入变化引起的输出改变幅度，但相同幅度可能对应不同的语义行为（支持事实-反事实差异、反对它、或在路径中出现但终点消失），缺乏对反应意义的解释能力。

Method: 通过逐步揭示配对输入（事实与反事实）的对比发展轨迹，利用终点对比来解读路径；将对齐、反对和终点无效的响应分别归为证据 E、矛盾 C 和脆弱性 F，满足 Abs = E + C + F 的严格分解，且在终点相对公理下唯一。

Result: 在受控视觉和表格实验中三个分量与独立测量的行为一致；72 模型 ImageNet-9 审计中，DECAF 最大分量与观察行为的一致率达 96.4%（仅用幅度时为 35.0%）；改变揭示路径可使总响应提升近 80%，但证据几乎不变而脆弱性增长 4 倍以上；在 FunnyBirds 和 ImageNet-1k 上，短前向 DECAF 轨迹优于通用归因基线；在 1B 级 DINOv2 上以 4.75 倍更短时间和 2.36 倍更低峰值内存匹配强梯度基线。

Conclusion: DECAF 分解能区分幅度的语义含义，提供比纯幅度更准确的解释，且计算高效，适用于大规模模型。

Abstract: Perturbation methods explain model decisions by measuring prediction changes under altered inputs, but response magnitude tells us only how much a model reacts, not what that reaction means. The same magnitude can support the final factual-counterfactual difference, oppose it, or arise strongly along the perturbation path yet vanish at the endpoint. We therefore track how the contrast develops as paired inputs are progressively revealed, using the final contrast to interpret the trajectory. We introduce DECAF (Decomposition of Evidence, Contradiction, And Fragility), which routes aligned, opposed, and endpoint-null responses into evidence E, contradiction C, and fragility F. The decomposition preserves ordinary magnitude exactly, Abs = E + C + F, and is unique under endpoint-relative axioms. Across controlled vision and tabular settings, the three components track independently measured behavior. In a 72-model ImageNet-9 audit, we compare cases with nearly identical response magnitude but different independently measured behaviors. The largest DECAF component agrees with an observed behavior in 96.4% of cases, compared with 35.0% for magnitude alone. Changing only the reveal path increases total response by nearly 80%, yet evidence barely changes while fragility grows by more than 4x. On FunnyBirds and ImageNet-1k, short forward-only DECAF trajectories outperform the tested general-purpose attribution baselines. On a 1B-scale DINOv2 model, a short trajectory matches a strong gradient-based baseline with 4.75x lower wall time and 2.36x lower peak memory.

</details>


### [209] [Moose: Latent concept learning with reasoning-shortcut awareness in $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12961)
*Olga Mashkova,Asaad Mohammedsaleh,Fernando Zhapa-Camacho,Robert Hoehndorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Moose 方法，将 OWL 2 EL 本体（EL++ TBox 和有限 ABox）编译为可微分的加权模型计数层（Sentential Decision Diagram），通过添加闭包子句克服 EL++ 在部分监督下的表达限制，并首次在 OWL EL 设置中正式定义部分监督的潜在概念学习任务；实验表明优于命题式神经符号方法、模糊逻辑和本体嵌入基线，并提供了首个 OWL EL 设置中的推理捷径分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OWL 2 EL 被用于大型生产本体（如 Gene Ontology 和 SNOMED CT），但现有神经符号方法仅支持命题理论或 Datalog，且未在本体设置中研究推理捷径问题。需要一种能处理 EL++ 本体并支持部分监督的神经符号学习方法，同时具备推理捷径感知能力。

Method: 将 EL++ TBox 和有限 ABox 编译为可微的加权模型计数层（Sentential Decision Diagram, SDD），并在声明的穷举族上添加 EL++ 之外的闭包子句以增强表达力；证明该编译过程的终止性、正确性、完备性和多项式中间大小，并在 Lean 中验证；基于此定义第一个正式的 OWL EL 部分监督潜在概念学习任务。

Result: 在 MNIST-with-ontology 和 Pizzaïolo 数据集上，Moose 优于基于命题神经符号、模糊逻辑和本体嵌入的基线方法；首次在 OWL EL 设置中进行了推理捷径分析。

Conclusion: Moose 成功将 EL++ 本体编译为可微 SDD，为 OWL EL 本体提供了一种有效的神经符号学习方法，能够处理部分监督学习并感知推理捷径，推动了本体与深度学习结合的研究。

Abstract: The OWL 2 EL profile is used in some of the largest production ontologies, including the Gene Ontology and SNOMED CT. Existing neuro-symbolic (NeSy) learning methods accept propositional theories or Datalog, and reasoning-shortcut (RS) awareness has not been investigated in ontology settings. We present Moose, a method that compiles an $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$ TBox and finite ABox to a Sentential Decision Diagram (SDD). The SDD acts as a differentiable weighted-model-counting layer, and we add closure clauses outside the $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$ profile on declared exhaustive families to overcome the limited expressivity of $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$ under partial supervision. We show termination, soundness, completeness, and polynomial intermediate sizes, and validate the proofs in Lean. We then define the first formal partial-supervision latent-concept-learning task over an OWL EL ontology, i.e., learning per-individual classifiers for latent concepts from observed ABox literals, and evaluate Moose on MNIST-with-ontology and Pizzaïolo. Moose improves over propositional-NeSy, fuzzy-logic, and ontology embedding baselines, and presents the first reasoning-shortcut analysis in an OWL EL setting.

</details>


### [210] [OGR-MARL: Option-Guided Residual Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Heterogeneous USV Cooperative Pursuit in Constrained Port Waterways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12995)
*Mao Jiayang,Wang Lanfeng,Peng Zhao-Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OGR-MARL框架，一种选项引导的残差多智能体强化学习框架，用于异构USV在受限港口航道中的协同追捕。该框架可解耦于具体MARL算法，集成共享逃逸信念、角色条件选项目标、自适应规则惩罚和残差策略学习。在MADDPG、MATD3、MAPPO、MASAC上实例化，其中OGR-MASAC在夏子门港口航道场景中达到75%捕获率，并零样本迁移至QGIS/AIS地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决异构USV在受限港口航道中协同追捕逃逸者时面临的导航约束、交通约束和角色约束问题，传统方法需要从零探索受限环境，效率低且难以保证规则合规性。

Method: 提出OGR-MARL框架，包含四个核心组件：共享逃逸信念（shared evader belief）、角色条件选项目标（role-conditioned option targets）、自适应规则惩罚（adaptive rule penalties）和残差策略学习（residual policy learning）。该框架允许不同MARL算法在规则引导行为之上学习修正动作，而非从零探索。在MADDPG、MATD3、MAPPO、MASAC四种连续控制MARL骨干上实例化。

Result: 在抽象夏子门港口航道场景中，OGR-MASAC实例化达到75%捕获率，在任务有效的规则合规性和异构协调性方面表现最佳。无需重新训练即可零样本迁移至QGIS/AIS信息构建的夏子门地图，展示出在更复杂港口场景中的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: OGR-MARL框架通过将规则引导行为与残差学习结合，有效提升了异构USV在受限港口航道中的协同追捕性能，且具有良好的泛化能力，为实际港口水域的USV协同作业提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous USV cooperative pursuit in constrained port waterways requires evader interception under navigation, traffic, and role constraints. This paper proposes OGR-MARL, an option-guided residual multi-agent reinforcement learning framework that is decoupled from a specific MARL algorithm. OGR-MARL integrates shared evader belief, role-conditioned option targets, adaptive rule penalties, and residual policy learning, allowing different MARL algorithms to learn corrective actions on top of rule-guided behaviors rather than exploring constrained port environments from scratch. We instantiate OGR-MARL with representative continuous-control MARL backbones, including MADDPG, MATD3, MAPPO, and MASAC, yielding OGR-MADDPG, OGR-MATD3, OGR-MAPPO, and OGR-MASAC. Experiments in an abstract Xiazhimen port-waterway scenario show that the OGR-MASAC instantiation achieves a 75.0% capture rate, promising mission-effective rule compliance, and the best heterogeneous coordination among the tested methods. Without retraining, zero-shot transfer to a QGIS/AIS-informed Xiazhimen map achieves promising results, demonstrating the generalization potential of OGR-MARL in more complex port scenarios.

</details>


### [211] [Foundations of MT-PDCL: Measure-Theoretic Probabilistic Definite Clause Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13018)
*Costin Bădică,Amelia Bădică*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MT-PDCL extends probabilistic logic programming to arbitrary continuous domains by replacing discrete propositional grounding with Lebesgue integration over Borel measure spaces, enabling exact, differentiable inference while preserving the declarative syntax of definite clause logic.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard probabilistic logic programming frameworks restrict exact inference to finite domains and discrete distributions because they rely on grounding programs into discrete propositional representations. MT-PDCL eliminates this finite-domain restriction.

Method: The framework explicitly defines stochastic variables over bounded index domains, equips the interpretation space with standard Borel σ-algebras, and builds on Continuous Distribution Semantics. Declarative entailment is defined via exact Lebesgue integration over the continuous measure space, and a continuous immediate consequence operator is introduced to unify integration of continuous priors with evaluation of continuous observations.

Result: The approach replaces the combinatorial bottleneck of discrete grounding with exact, algebraic, and structurally differentiable inference. While it trades discrete combinatorics for the geometric curse of dimensionality, it achieves the expressive power of continuous probabilistic models.

Conclusion: MT-PDCL achieves the expressive power of continuous probabilistic models while preserving the pure declarative syntax of definite clause logic, thus providing a foundational framework for exact inference over continuous measurable spaces.

Abstract: Standard probabilistic logic programming frameworks typically rely on grounding logic programs into discrete propositional representations. This operational requirement restricts exact inference to finite domains and discrete probability distributions. In this paper, we introduce Measure-Theoretic Probabilistic Definite Clause Logic (MT-PDCL), a generalized foundational framework that eliminates this finite-domain restriction. By explicitly defining stochastic variables over bounded index domains and equipping the interpretation space with standard Borel $σ$-algebras, MT-PDCL allows logical variables to operate natively over continuous measurable spaces. Building on Continuous Distribution Semantics, MT-PDCL models probabilistic rules as mutually independent causal events. However, rather than aggregating these derivations via finite boolean circuits, declarative entailment is formally defined through exact Lebesgue integration over the continuous measure space. We introduce a continuous immediate consequence operator that unifies the integration of continuous prior distributions with the evaluation of exact continuous observations. We demonstrate that this approach replaces the combinatorial bottleneck of discrete grounding with exact, algebraic, and structurally differentiable inference. While this transition trades discrete combinatorics for the geometric curse of dimensionality, it achieves the expressive power of continuous probabilistic models while preserving the pure declarative syntax of definite clause logic.

</details>


### [212] [From Local Mismatch to Global Impact: Optimizing Cache Reuse Policy for Efficient Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13043)
*Xichen Ye,Yifan Wu,Zhikang Xie,Xiangyu Yue,Cheng Jin,Weizhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出全局影响缓存（GCache），通过将缓存策略搜索重构为双层优化问题，并利用Bernstein形式重新参数化误差传播指数，从而在理论上对齐误差加权函数与生成质量损失，实现扩散模型的高效加速，同时保持甚至提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于缓存的加速策略依赖局部相似性启发式，与最终生成质量严重错位，导致非均匀误差传播和累积，影响视觉保真度。

Method: 建立误差传播上界的理论刻画，并使用Bernstein形式重新参数化传播指数；将缓存策略搜索转化为双层优化问题，内层目标寻找最优重用策略，外层目标对齐误差加权函数与生成质量损失。

Result: 在视频和图像生成任务上，GCache一致优于先前缓存策略。在Wan2.1视频扩散模型上实现2.17倍加速，同时LPIPS从0.1095降至0.0316，显著提升生成质量。

Conclusion: GCache有效调和了理论严谨性与经验性能，通过学习优先计算对视觉保真度影响最大的部分，在加速推理的同时提升生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved dominant performance in visual generation but suffer from substantial inference overhead. While cache-based acceleration has emerged as a promising solution, existing policies rely on local similarity heuristics, which we identify as being significantly misaligned with final generation quality. This discrepancy stems from the non-uniform propagation and accumulation of errors along the denoising trajectory. To address this, we propose Global-Impact Cache (GCache). We first establish a rigorous theoretical characterization of the error propagation upper bound. Recognizing that this bound can be overly conservative for complex, highly non-convex diffusion models, we further reparameterize the propagation exponent with a Bernstein form and reformulate cache policy search as a bilevel optimization problem. In detail, GCache identifies an optimal reuse policy in the inner objective while aligning the error-weighting function with generation quality loss in the outer objective. This framework effectively reconciles theoretical rigor with empirical performance, learning to prioritize computation where it most impacts visual fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GCache consistently outperforms prior caching strategies on both video and image generation. Notably, on the state-of-the-art Wan2.1 video diffusion model, GCache maintains a 2.17x speedup while significantly enhancing generation quality, reducing LPIPS from 0.1095 to 0.0316.

</details>


### [213] [BoardroomAI: Dependency-Aware Human-Steerable Multi-Agent Deliberation through Evolving Decision Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13046)
*Sanjeev Manivannan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BoardroomAI 将人类作为持续参与者，允许在决策过程中随时干预（如质疑假设、修改约束等），通过类型化决策图、干预编译器、依赖感知传播和评估框架，实现人机协同的多智能体决策。实验表明，该方法在保证决策有效性的同时大幅减少计算开销，但在部分案例中因上下文不足而无法合成更新决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于转录的多智能体系统中，人类仅提供初始问题后被动等待最终答案，无法在智能体内部推理过程中实时调整证据、约束或优先级。现实组织决策需要人机持续协同，因此需要一种支持人类动态干预的决策框架。

Method: 提出 BoardroomAI 框架，包含四个组件：(i) 类型化决策图，表示证据、假设、约束、主张、反对意见、备选方案、风险、决策、语义依赖及专家责任；(ii) 干预编译器，将人类确认的操作转换为显式图更新；(iii) 依赖感知传播，识别受影响的子图、保留未影响部分并选择性地重新激活相关专家；(iv) 评估框架，衡量干预影响、修复覆盖率、保留率、重新计算量和决策有效性。

Result: 在 600 个生成的决策 DAG 干预实验中，传播方法与穷举影响计算匹配，但仅检查了 14.59% 的节点。在 12 个案例的探索性试点中，选择性修复重新计算了 62.11% 的金标准节点，保留了所有金标准未影响节点，并在 6 个案例中产生有效的更新决策，其余 6 个案例则放弃决策。

Conclusion: 结果表明，正确的干预路由仍可能因上下文不足而无法合成决策，因此需要“决策充分上下文封闭”来支持人类引导的多智能体协商。所有结果均为合成数据与原型级别，需进一步验证。

Abstract: Organizational decisions are co-created while evidence, constraints, and human priorities continue to evolve. In conventional transcript-based multi-agent systems, humans typically provide an initial problem, agents deliberate internally, and the system returns a final response. BoardroomAI instead treats the human as a persistent participant who can intervene by challenging assumptions, modifying constraints, changing priorities, introducing evidence, or redirecting the decision process. We operationalize this human--agent coexistence through four components: (i) a typed decision graph representing evidence, assumptions, constraints, claims, objections, alternatives, risks, decisions, semantic dependencies, and specialist responsibility; (ii) an intervention compiler that converts confirmed human actions into explicit graph updates; (iii) dependency-aware propagation that identifies affected subgraphs, preserves unaffected artifacts, and selectively reactivates relevant specialists; and (iv) an evaluation framework measuring intervention impact, repair coverage, preservation, recomputation, and decision validity. Across 600 generated decision-DAG interventions, propagation matched exhaustive impact computation while inspecting only 14.59% of nodes. In a 12-case exploratory pilot, selective repair recomputed 62.11% of canonical nodes, preserved all gold-unaffected nodes, and produced valid updated decisions in six cases while abstaining in the remaining six. These abstentions show that correct intervention routing may still provide insufficient context for synthesis, motivating a \emph{decision-sufficient context closure} for human-steered multi-agent deliberation. All results are synthetic and prototype-level.

</details>


### [214] [DMDIntel: Interpreting Large Language Models via Dynamic Mode Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13048)
*Amogh Joshi,Animesh Mukherjee,Sergey Utyuzhnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DMDIntel使用动态模式分解（DMD）对LLM在分类任务中的预测进行可解释性分析，通过分解隐藏状态为突出模式（模态），并基于投影值对输入标记进行排序归因，在三个数据集和三个模型家族上的实验表明其性能远超PCA、集成梯度和SHAP等现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM可解释性方法（如PCA、集成梯度和SHAP）在输入归因方面性能有限，需要一种更有效的技术来使LLM的分类预测变得可解释。

Method: DMDIntel基于动态模式分解（DMD）构建输入归因流程：首先将LLM的隐藏状态分解为突出模式（模态），然后根据输入标记在这些模式上的投影值对其进行排序归因。

Result: 在三个数据集和三个模型家族上的严格实验表明，DMDIntel获得的输入标记排序归因结果显著优于PCA、集成梯度和SHAP等最先进技术。

Conclusion: DMDIntel是一种有效的LLM可解释性方法，通过动态模式分解实现了更优的输入归因性能，为理解LLM分类决策提供了新的途径。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce DMDIntel which uses dynamic mode decomposition (DMD) to make the predictions made by LLMs in a classification task interpretable. It develops an input attribution pipeline, that first decomposes the hidden states of an LLM into prominent patterns, also known as modes, and then associates ranks to the input tokens based on the projection values on those modes. Rigorous experiments across three datasets and three model families consistently show that the ranked attribution of input tokens obtained using DMDIntel by far outperforms state-of-the-art techniques such as principal component analysis, integrated gradients and SHAP.

</details>


### [215] [VALG: An Agentic System for ML Theory Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13060)
*Dechen Zhang,Xuan Tang,Xinxiang Yin,Xingwu Chen,Jian Qian,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VALG是一个自主智能体系统，用于机器学习理论研究。它结合了多层次验证、自适应性学习理论问题公式化和图结构证明开发，能够在证明失败时识别障碍来源并动态调整问题表述或证明策略。在五个COLT 2026开放问题的九个子问题上评估，其中两个生成了与源问题范围匹配的最终定理候选，其余产生了限制方法结果、特例或条件定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习理论研究通常需要数学设定（数据模型、训练协议、损失、度量等）来定义现象并证明定理。解决开放问题要求问题表述、定理目标和证明机制协同发展。研究者提出假设，通过初步理论或实验分析测试，然后完善假设和证明。本文研究能否将此过程组织为自主智能体工作流，即自动化ML理论研究的闭环流程。

Method: VALG采用三层架构：（1）多层次验证（Verification），确保逻辑正确性；（2）自适应问题公式化（Adaptive formulation），当证明受阻时动态调整定理表述或提出相关变体/松弛版本；（3）图结构证明开发（Graph-structured proof），维护类型化的证明依赖图，按依赖顺序构建和审核局部证明。当证明尝试失败时，系统自动区分障碍来自推导细节、证明结构还是问题表述本身，并据此调整下一步策略。若障碍在表述层面，则生成明确相关的变体或松弛问题，保持与新定理的数学关系。

Result: 在五个COLT 2026开放问题的九个子问题上测试：两个运行产生了与源问题描述范围匹配的内部最终定理候选；其余七个产生了限制方法的结果、特例或条件定理。这些案例研究表明VALG能够保持源范围匹配、松弛版本、条件结果和受阻尝试之间的数学区分性。

Conclusion: VALG展示了将ML理论研究的探索过程（问题表述、证明构造、失败分析与适应性调整）自动化组织为智能体工作流的可行性。它能够系统性地生成不同粒度的理论结果，并在数学上明确区分不同类型的中间结果。代码已开源，为后续ML理论自动研究工具的开发提供了基础。

Abstract: Machine learning theory studies learning procedures through mathematical setups in which the data model, training protocol, oracle access, loss, metric, and randomness define the phenomenon that a theorem is meant to explain. Solving an open problem therefore requires the problem formulation, theorem target, and proof mechanism to be developed in concert. Researchers formulate hypotheses, test them through preliminary theoretical or empirical analysis, and refine both assumptions and proofs. We investigate whether this process can be organized as an autonomous agentic workflow for ML theory research.
  We develop VALG, an agentic system that combines multi-level Verification, Adaptive formulation of Learning-theory problems, and Graph-structured proof development. Within each source-relative theorem branch, VALG maintains a fixed mathematical specification, checks the theorem-level composition of a typed proof-dependency graph, and constructs and reviews local proofs in dependency order. When a proof attempt fails, VALG identifies whether the obstruction lies in a derivation, the proof structure, or the theorem formulation and routes the next attempt accordingly. Formulation-level obstructions initiate an explicitly related variant or relaxation, preserving the mathematical relation between the resulting theorem and the source problem.
  We evaluate VALG on nine subproblems from five COLT 2026 open problems. Two runs produce internally finalized theorem candidates that match the scope of their source briefs; the remaining seven yield restricted-method results, special cases, or conditional theorems. These case studies show how VALG keeps source-scope matches, relaxations, conditional results, and blocked attempts mathematically distinct. VALG is open source at https://github.com/DechenZhang/VALG-ML-Theory-Agent.

</details>


### [216] [Uniform Herding: Exemplar Replay with Representation Refresh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13061)
*Krishna Subedi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Uniform Herding方法，在类增量学习中通过均匀分配活跃样本集和刷新机制，在CIFAR-100上以ResNet-18骨干网络取得44.00%准确率和17.22%遗忘率，优于iCaRL的42.33%和24.87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回放在类增量学习中必须保留旧类，但仅有有限的有界活跃样本集可用于回放。现有方法（如iCaRL）在特征表示变化时，回放样本可能过时，导致遗忘增加。需要一种更有效的样本分配和刷新策略来保持代表性。

Method: Uniform Herding：将当前活跃集均匀分配到所有已观察类别，并使用有界候选池刷新选定样本，使其适应当前特征表示。在CIFAR-100上使用ResNet-18，活跃预算M=2000，检索预算b=64进行实验。

Result: Uniform Herding在CIFAR-100十任务类增量学习中取得44.00±0.51%最终平均准确率和17.22±0.43%遗忘率，优于iCaRL的42.33±1.20%和24.87±1.11%。在Uniform Herding协议内，替换NME或herding会降低准确率，移除蒸馏会增加遗忘；改变检索预算影响小于改变活跃预算。

Conclusion: Uniform Herding通过均匀分配和候选池刷新有效提升了类增量学习的性能，但端到端比较未隔离刷新效果，且结果限于特定协议。

Abstract: As the feature representation changes, replay must preserve the earlier classes. However, only a bounded active exemplar set can be replayed. We propose Uniform Herding, which allocates the current active set across observed classes and uses a bounded candidate pool to refresh their chosen exemplars in the current representation. On CIFAR-100 with ten class-incremental tasks, a ResNet-18 backbone, active budget $M=2{,}000$, retrieval budget $b=64$, and three seeds, Uniform Herding obtains $44.00\pm0.51\%$ final average accuracy and $17.22\pm0.43\%$ forgetting, compared with $42.33\pm1.20\%$ and $24.87\pm1.11\%$ for iCaRL. Within the Uniform Herding protocol, final accuracy decreased when NME or herding was replaced with the tested alternatives, while forgetting increased when distillation was removed. Changing the retrieval budget has a smaller effect across the tested range than changing the active budget. The comparison with iCaRL is end-to-end. It does not isolate the effect of refresh from the other protocol differences. These results are limited to the tested protocol.

</details>


### [217] [Explanatory Engagement Under Rare Anomalous Failure: Asymptotic Rarity in Model Behavior (or: The Asymptotic AI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13063)
*Sam Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探究了在大语言模型的工作流中，当工具调用失败概率从0.2降至0.0001时，模型解释性参与（长度、特异性、自信度）的变化模式。通过三种开源模型、五种提示条件，发现总体趋势下解释长度单调递减，但按条件细分后，强制即时解释条件下出现预期中的上升随后稳定，而非崩溃；未提示条件下发现了模型特定的自我监控行为。提示结构是崩溃可见性的首要调节因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作关注模型是否注意到异常，本研究进一步追问：在低且可控的失败率工作流中，随着失败变得极其罕见，模型的解释性参与（长度、特异性、自信度）如何变化？旨在理解模型在极端稀疏异常下的行为边界。

Method: 使用三种开源权重模型（qwen3:8b, llama3.1:8b, mistral:7b）在本地零成本环境中执行重复工具调用任务，其中一次调用以概率p失败，p从0.2到0.0001共8个速率。在五种提示条件（从立即提示到无提示）下进行实验。还包括一个保证失败的对照运行（72个单元，填补随机抽样未产生真实失败的速率点）。

Result: 总体聚合下，解释长度呈平坦单调递减。按条件拆分后：在立即强制解释条件下，预测的上升被确认，但随后是平台期而非崩溃，长度在p=0.05时达到峰值28.4词，最罕见速率下稳定在17.4-19.0词，自信度从约53%上升到70%-90%。在分组运行条件下，未出现崩溃。在被动无提示条件下，聚合量级是地板伪影，但恢复的日志缺口揭示了模型特定的自我监控：llama3.1:8b主动提供结构化自信报告，有时随着试验累积侵蚀自身自信；其他两个模型仅以模板方式提供一次。

Conclusion: 提示结构是崩溃可见性的首要调节因素；模型在是否识别异常方面存在差异，这与识别后参与度不同。局限性：离散速率点无法捕捉速率之间的行为，需未来研究。

Abstract: Prior work on LLM behavior under anomalous conditions asks whether a model notices anomalies. We ask a narrower question: once a model sits in a workflow with a low, controllable failure rate, does its explanatory engagement - length, specificity, self-reported confidence - change as failure grows asymptotically rarer? We built a local, zero-cost harness on three open-weight models (qwen3:8b, llama3.1:8b, mistral:7b) running a repeated tool-call task where one call fails at probability p, swept across eight rates from 0.2 to 0.0001, under five elicitation conditions from immediate prompting to none. We hypothesized a rise in engagement as failures grew rarer, then a collapse near a detectability threshold. Pooled across conditions this appeared false: length fell in a flat, monotonic pattern. Splitting by condition overturned that. Under immediate_forced, where the model must explain every failure instantly, the predicted rise is confirmed but followed by a plateau, not a collapse: length peaks at 28.4 words at p=0.05, settles to 17.4-19.0 words at the rarest rates, and confidence rises unevenly from about 53% to the 70s-90s. Under grouped_runs, explanation batched to run-end, no collapse appears. Under passive_unprompted, aggregate magnitude is a floor artifact, but a recovered logging gap revealed real, model-specific self-monitoring: llama3.1:8b volunteers structured confidence reports unprompted, sometimes eroding its own confidence as trials accumulate; the other two do so only once, as boilerplate. Elicitation structure is a first-class moderator of collapse observability. A companion guaranteed-failure run (72 cells, backfilling rates where random sampling gave zero real failures) shows models differ in whether they recognize an anomaly, distinct from engagement once recognized. Limitation: discrete rate points cannot capture behavior between them, a direction for future work.

</details>


### [218] [Behavioral Reprogramming of Open-Weights Models: Cognitive Plasticity and Alignment Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13069)
*Lucia Malíčková*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模并行超参数扫描（405个HPC任务）和LoRA参数高效微调，将开源大语言模型从被动的谄媚式助手转变为主动的苏格拉底式对话框架，确定了LoRA秩r=16为架构阈值，最优训练轮次e∈[2,3]（最小验证损失0.919），14B参数模型困惑度降至1.414，并通过直接偏好优化（DPO）解耦断言行为与局部语法，跨语言压力测试揭示了零-shot人格迁移的能力与退化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战大语言模型默认的被动、谄媚式对齐范式，通过行为重编程诱导主动、苏格拉底式的对话框架，在高性能计算约束下实现高频提问生成，并建立跨语言行为修改的实证框架。

Method: 采用大规模并行化超参数扫描（405个HPC作业）定义参数高效微调（PEFT）的数学边界；通过LoRA秩消融实验确定架构阈值r=16；进行轮次消融找到最优训练窗口e∈[2,3]；扩展模型容量至14B参数；应用直接偏好优化（DPO）解耦断言行为与局部语法；进行跨语言压力测试评估零-shot人格迁移。

Result: LoRA秩r=16为架构阈值；最优训练窗口e∈[2,3]（依数据集密度而定，最小验证损失0.919）；14B参数模型评估困惑度降至1.414；DPO成功解耦断言行为；跨语言测试显示相近语系中鲁棒对齐，形态学远距离目标中存在可识别的退化路径。

Conclusion: 建立了计算高效的跨语言行为修改的严格实证框架，实现了从被动助手到主动苏格拉底式人格的转变，并揭示了零-shot人格迁移在跨语言场景下的能力与结构边界。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are predominantly aligned to function as passive, sycophantic assistants. We challenge this default paradigm by empirically evaluating the cognitive plasticity of open-weight architectures when subjected to rigorous behavioral reprogramming. Our objective is to induce a proactive, Socratic conversational framework, characterized by high-frequency question generation under strictly constrained high-performance computing (HPC) conditions. Through a massively parallelized hyperparameter sweep comprising 405 HPC jobs, we define precise mathematical bounds for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT). We identify an architectural threshold at LoRA rank $r=16$ and demonstrate via extensive epoch ablation that generalization capacity strictly reaches its optimal convergence within an optimized training window of $e \in [2, 3]$ depending on dataset density (minimum validation loss of 0.919). Furthermore, scaling model capacity to 14B parameters yielded a lower localized evaluation perplexity (1.414). Subsequent Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) successfully decoupled the underlying assertive behavior from localized syntax, while rigorous cross-lingual stress testing reveals both the capabilities and the structural boundaries of zero-shot persona transfer, demonstrating robust alignment in closely related linguistic families alongside identifiable degradation pathways in morphologically distant targets. These findings establish a rigorous empirical framework for compute-efficient, cross-lingual behavioral modification.

</details>


### [219] [EEG-PRIME: Prototype-Aligned Representation Learning with Multi-Level Conditioning for EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13072)
*Shuailei Zhang,Muyun Jiang,Wei Zhang,Jinbo Chen,Zhiwei Guo,Yong Li,Yi Ding,Cuntai Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EEG-PRIME，一个两阶段EEG基础模型，通过掩码预训练和原型对齐指令调优，实现跨数据集多任务解码，在16个数据集上超越基线，并展示出零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG解码模型因采集协议和个体神经生理的域偏移而难以跨数据集和跨主体泛化。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 预训练阶段：EEG编码器通过带频率截止频谱增强的掩码重建学习可迁移表示；2) 指令调优阶段：结合任务语义、数据集特定和主体不变条件，通过层查询调制（Layer-wise Query Modulation）调节Q-Former，并使用冻结的类别标签文本嵌入作为原型进行余弦相似度预测。

Result: 在16个数据集（涵盖运动想象、情绪识别、ADHD检测、隐蔽言语和脑力负荷）上，跨主体设置下一致优于SOTA基线和之前的EEG基础模型。在额外两个保留数据集上，无需目标域优化、校准或线性探针，即可达到与内会话校准模型相当的平衡准确率，展现零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: EEG-PRIME通过结合掩码预训练和指令调优，实现了指令感知和主体不变的跨数据集解码，具备强大的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) decoding models often generalize poorly across datasets and subjects due to domain shifts in acquisition protocols and individual neurophysiology. We propose EEG-PRIME, a two-stage EEG foundation model for cross-dataset multi-task decoding. EEG-PRIME combines masked pretraining with prototype-aligned instruction tuning to enable instruction-aware and subject-invariant decoding across diverse BCI paradigms. During pretraining, an EEG encoder learns transferable representations through masked reconstruction with frequency-cutoff spectral augmentation. During instruction tuning, EEG-PRIME incorporates task-semantic, dataset-specific, and subject-invariant conditioning. The resulting conditioning signal modulates the Q-Former through Layer-wise Query Modulation, while frozen text embeddings of class labels serve as prototypes for cosine-similarity-based prediction across heterogeneous label spaces. Experiments on sixteen datasets covering motor imagery, emotion recognition, ADHD detection, covert speech, and mental workload show consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines and prior EEG foundation models under cross-subject settings. On two additional held-out datasets, EEG-PRIME achieves balanced accuracy comparable to within-session calibration models without target-domain optimization, calibration, or linear probing, demonstrating promising zero-shot transfer capability.

</details>


### [220] [SPADE: Speculative Decoding for Precise and Low Cost Distributed Edge Cloud Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13076)
*Divya Jyoti Bajpai,Kishan Kumar Upadhyay,Manjesh Kumar Hanawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种边缘-云协同的投机解码推理框架，在边缘部署小型草稿模型快速生成候选token，云端大型验证模型并行验证，仅拒绝时纠正，从而减少76%的云端调用且保持精度无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署受限于高计算需求：边缘部署小模型精度下降，云端部署大模型每token计算成本高。需要一种既保持大模型精度又降低推理成本和云端开销的方法。

Method: 采用投机解码（Speculative Decoding），边缘端部署紧凑草稿模型快速生成候选token序列，云端部署大型验证模型并行验证；接受的token保留，仅被拒绝的token触发验证模型纠正；无需重新训练，即插即用。

Result: 在SpecBench和CNN/Dailymail数据集上的多个NLP任务中，云端模型调用减少76%，且精度与完整大模型相比无损失。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了可扩展、成本高效且准确的大语言模型边缘-云协同部署，为实际环境中的LLM应用提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success in natural language understanding and generation, but their deployment is constrained by high computational demands. Deploying smaller LLMs directly on the edge can circumvent this, but with degraded accuracy. Deploying smaller cloud-based big LLMs preserves performance, but at the cost of expensive per-token computation. We present a distributed inference framework, \our{}, that integrates speculative decoding (SD) across edge and cloud. A compact draft model deployed on the edge generates candidate tokens rapidly, and a large verifier model on the cloud validates these tokens in parallel. Accepted tokens are retained, while only rejections trigger verifier correction, substantially reducing the number of cloud queries. Our plug-and-play design shifts the bulk of computation to the edge, significantly lowers inference time and cloud cost, and preserves the accuracy of the big model without any retraining requirement. Our approach demonstrates a practical path toward scalable, cost-efficient, and accurate deployment of LLMs in real-world environments. Experimental results across multiple Natural Language Processing tasks using SpecBench and CNN/Dailymail datasets demonstrate that \our{} reduces the cloud model calls by $76\%$ with zero loss in accuracy as compared to the full model.

</details>


### [221] [Multi-Layer Context Camouflaging: A Semantic Superposition and Contextual Lamination Framework for Malpractice-Resilient Online Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13100)
*Gupta Lovi Raj,Kaur Kamalpreet,Dama Sri Ram,Parani Prajithaa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多层上下文伪装理论（MCCT），通过语义叠加将真实评估内容与合成伪装统一渲染，利用条件熵量化计算模糊性，在保护内容免受截图、OCR等提取攻击的同时，保证合法用户的可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字评估内容面临屏幕截图、屏幕共享、光学字符识别和自动抓取等提取攻击威胁，现有防护手段缺乏数学严谨性。亟需一个既能抵御对抗性提取又能保持合法用户可读性的理论框架。

Method: 基于MARS框架扩展MSCCM，提出MCCT。形式化定义提取通道算子，构建六个耦合构造：上下文反转算子、上下文层压算子、分离通道、人类可读性泛函、计算模糊性泛函和上下文伪装张量。利用条件熵建立计算模糊性的闭环表达式，通过精确滤波恒等式确保合法恢复，并推导模糊性、伪装密度、语义保留、多观测泄露和时间复用等理论性质。

Result: 给出了具有明确计算复杂度的渲染算法和预注册评估协议，理论分析证明了MCCT在行为适应性、可访问性和计算韧性方面的数学基础，为安全数字评估提供了可验证的保障。

Conclusion: MCCT为数字评估内容保护提供了数学上严格的框架，能够有效抵抗多种提取攻击，同时不牺牲合法用户的阅读体验，推动实现行为自适应、可访问且计算上具有韧性的评估系统。

Abstract: Contemporary online assessment systems rely primarily on browser lockdown, webcam monitoring, and behavioural analytics, yet remain vulnerable to attacks that extract the assessment content itself through screenshots, screen sharing, optical character recognition, and automated scraping. This paper extends the Multi-dimensional Spatio-Temporal Context Camouflaging Model (MSCCM) within the MARS (Multi-modal Assessment Resilience Suite) by introducing the Multi-Layer Context Camouflaging Theory (MCCT), a mathematical framework that protects rendered assessment content through semantic superposition. Authentic assessment content and synthetically generated camouflage are represented as a unified rendering while remaining recoverable only by legitimate candidates. The framework models the adversarial extraction process through an explicit extraction-channel operator and develops six coupled constructs: the Context Inversion Operator, Contextual Lamination Operator, Separation Channel, Human Readability Functional, Computational Ambiguity Functional, and Context Camouflage Tensor. Computational ambiguity is formulated using conditional entropy, yielding a closed-form expression that quantifies uncertainty during unauthorized extraction, while legitimate recovery is guaranteed through an exact filtering identity. We further establish theoretical properties governing ambiguity, camouflage density, semantic preservation, multi-observation leakage, and temporal multiplexing, and present a rendering algorithm with computational complexity and a pre-registered evaluation protocol. MCCT provides a mathematically rigorous foundation for behaviorally adaptive, accessibility-aware, and computationally resilient digital assessment by securing rendered assessment content while preserving readability for legitimate users.

</details>


### [222] [Robust Dempster-Shafer Evidence Fusion with Chaos-Conflict Measurement and Historical-Experience Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13108)
*Huiyu Li,Weibo Liu,Xinru Xu,Dongchen Gao,Meng Zhang,Junhua Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Dempster-Shafer理论的多源证据融合框架，通过混沌-冲突度量联合量化证据间冲突与证据内非特异性，利用历史经验加权方案（谱聚类+后悔理论）评估长期可靠性，并结合自适应混合组合规则与信念区间决策策略，在16个数据集上平均F1=85.78、AUC=93.30，优于8种DST方法和3种梯度提升方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Dempster-Shafer证据融合方法存在两个局限：一是冲突度量将证据间不一致性与证据内不确定性分开评估，导致评估不完整；二是当前融合方法仅通过瞬时比较评估证据源，未利用其在多样化决策场景中的长期可靠性。

Method: 提出了一个统一的证据推理框架，包含：(1) 混沌-冲突度量，联合量化交叉证据冲突和证据内非特异性，并具备五个形式化性质保证；(2) 基于历史经验的加权方案，通过谱聚类划分决策空间并应用后悔理论计算上下文特定的可靠性档案；(3) 混合组合规则，根据全局冲突水平自适应平衡不确定性保留与加权共识；(4) 信念区间决策策略，在不丢弃认知不确定性的情况下实现鲁棒分类。

Result: 在16个真实世界基准数据集上，所提框架的平均F1分数为85.78，平均AUC为93.30，超越了8种基于DST的基线方法和3种梯度提升方法；消融分析验证了各组成部分的贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架为多源决策中的自适应证据融合提供了一种有效方法，通过联合度量冲突与不确定性、利用历史经验评估长期可靠性以及自适应融合策略，显著提升了融合性能。

Abstract: Multi-source evidence fusion under Dempster-Shafer theory faces two persistent challenges: existing conflict measures assess inter-evidence inconsistency and intra-evidence uncertainty independently, yielding incomplete evaluations, and current fusion methods evaluate evidence sources exclusively through instantaneous comparisns without exploiting their long-term reliability across diverse decision contexts. This paper proposes a unified evidence reasoning framework that addresses both limitations. Specifically, a chaos-conflict measurement is introduced to jointly quantify cross-evidence conflict and intra-evidence non-specificity, with five formally proven properties ensuring consistent assessment. A historical experience driven weighting scheme partitions the decision space via spectral clustering and applies regret theory to compute context-specific reliability profiles from past fusion outcomes. These mechanisms feed into a hybrid combination rule that adaptively balances uncertainty preservation against weighted consensus, controlled by the global conflict level, followed by a belief-interval decision strategy that enables robust classification without discarding epistemic uncertainty. Experiments on 16 real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves an average F1 score of 85.78 and a mean AUC of 93.30, outperforming eight DST-based baselines and three gradient boosting methods. Ablation analysis confirms the contribution of each component we proposed. The framework offers an effective approach for adaptive evidence fusion in multi-source decision making.

</details>


### [223] [SkillEvo: Self-Renewing Evolution Gradients from Multi-Turn Interaction Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13120)
*Qianxi Yan,Chunrong Chen,Jiuzhou Zhao,Min Zhang,Yongzhou Xu,Xiaochuan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillEvo，通过多轮用户模拟生成可信反馈梯度，并引入独立治理层主动修复退化，克服了现有Agent技能进化中单轮QA反馈梯度衰减、多轮缺陷不可见、结构退化无法定位修复等问题，在云服务场景下显著优于自反思和单轮QA驱动的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent技能生成缺乏闭环改进机制，即使有闭环也仅依赖单轮问答反馈，导致进化梯度快速衰减、多轮交互缺陷无法暴露、结构退化无法修复，进化停滞。

Method: SkillEvo包含两个组件：(1) 将多轮用户模拟从评估端点改造为反馈生成器，通过递进式追问逐层暴露缺陷；(2) 用独立治理层替代被动拒绝的标量门，主动修复事实退化与结构膨胀，防止梯度漂移。

Result: 在6类云服务、9个生产Skill、98个技能参考文件上，SkillEvo比自反思进化高出23.0个点，比单轮QA驱动进化高出15.4个点。

Conclusion: 技能持续进化的约束条件不在于编辑能力或迭代次数，而在于评估反馈能否持续提供可信的进化梯度；SkillEvo通过多层反馈生成与主动治理实现了更有效的进化。

Abstract: Agent Skills are today either hand-authored or produced in a single LLM generation pass, and consequently possess no closed loop through which they might improve from the interaction failures they actually cause. Recent work does close this loop, but derives its feedback from single-turn question-answering evaluation. The consequence is a sharp asymmetry: once the first round has patched the gaps that a single exchange can reveal, the evolution gradient decays, the defects that surface only across multiple turns remain invisible, and evolution stalls. Governance in these systems is likewise driven by an end-to-end verification score, a scalar gate that can reject a degraded candidate but can neither localize nor repair its structural cause. We argue that the binding constraint on sustained skill evolution is neither editing capability nor the number of iterations, but whether the evaluation feedback keeps supplying trustworthy evolution gradients. We introduce SkillEvo, in which trustworthy feedback generates the gradient and controllable governance constrains its direction. The first component recasts multi-turn user simulation from an evaluation endpoint into a feedback generator: follow-up questions expose defects layer by layer, so that every round of revision both consumes feedback and produces new feedback. The second replaces the passive rejection of a scalar gate with an independent governance layer that actively repairs factual degradation and structural bloat, preventing the gradient from drifting as degradation accumulates. Across six categories of cloud services, 9 production Skills, and 98 skill-reference files, SkillEvo surpasses self-reflection-based evolution by 23.0 points and single- turn-QA-driven evolution by 15.4 points.

</details>


### [224] [Numeracy in Large Language Models: Fundamental Limitations and Paths to Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13129)
*Aoxin Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出数值基础框架（NGF），将数值理解分解为表征基础（RG）和程序基础（PG），系统调查了LLM在基本数值任务（如大小比较、大整数运算、分数和科学记数法）上的失败模式、结构性解释和缓解策略，并对前沿模型进行了协调评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在数学推理基准上表现强劲，但在基本的数值任务上却不可靠，这种能力与高级数学推理不同，需要被独立研究。现有研究缺乏一个统一框架来系统性地理解LLM的数值基础能力。

Method: 提出数值基础框架（NGF），将数值能力分为表征基础（RG，将数字形式映射到数值、大小和等价表示）和程序基础（PG，按数学定义执行算术运算）。利用NGF组织诊断基准、失败模式、结构解释和缓解策略；在三个前沿模型家族上协调评估Number Cookbook、NumericBench和GSM-Symbolic；分析分词、位置编码、嵌入几何和预训练数据分布的影响。

Result: 发现LLM在原子数值任务、上下文数值任务和推理辅助数值任务上存在系统性失败；架构干预（如数字感知分词和Abacus Embeddings）可改善从头训练的模型，但对预训练系统的用户不可用；监督微调、推理支架和外部工具是更实际的方法。

Conclusion: 数值基础是LLM的一个独立能力维度，需要专门关注；提供了部署建议和未来研究方向，以实现基础模型中更可靠的数值行为。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong results on mathematical reasoning benchmarks yet remain unreliable on elementary numerical tasks, including magnitude comparison, large-integer arithmetic, fractions, and scientific notation. This survey examines basic numerical understanding as a capability distinct from high-level mathematical reasoning. We propose the Numerical Grounding Framework (NGF), which decomposes numeracy into Representational Grounding (RG), mapping numeral forms to value, magnitude, and equivalent representations, and Procedural Grounding (PG), executing arithmetic operations in accordance with their mathematical definitions. Using NGF, we organize recent diagnostic benchmarks, failure modes, structural explanations, and mitigation strategies. We review evidence concerning tokenization, positional encoding, embedding geometry, and pretraining-data distribution. We also apply NGF in a coordinated evaluation of three frontier model families across Number Cookbook, NumericBench, and GSM-Symbolic, comparing atomic, contextual, and reasoning-assisted numeracy. Architectural interventions such as digit-aware tokenization and Abacus Embeddings can improve models trained from scratch but are generally unavailable to users of pretrained systems, for whom supervised fine-tuning, reasoning scaffolds, and external tools are more practical. We conclude with deployment recommendations and research directions for more reliable numerical behavior in foundation models.

</details>


### [225] [Rethinking Normalization Placement for LLMs: Post-Norm under Curriculum Depth Growing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13156)
*Sheng Ren,Yadong Wang,Naiqiang Tan,Jiangang Kong,Jun Fang,Rui Liu,Jun Wang,Kai Chen,Lipeng Liang,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在基于课程深度增长的蒸馏训练中，后归一化（Post-norm）显著优于前归一化（Pre-norm），而在联合训练中两者无差异。实验表明归一化位置与训练课程应视为耦合设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在通过课程方式逐步增加深度（即深度增长课程）时，归一化层位置（Pre-norm vs Post-norm）对训练效果的影响，因为每个新添加的模块会接收已训练前缀产生的边界表示，这使得归一化位置与前向条件有关。

Method: 使用Qwen3-8B教师模型和9层学生模型进行受控蒸馏实验，对比联合训练（joint training）与课程增长（curriculum depth growth）两种设置下Pre-norm和Post-norm的表现，并通过单块冻结等对照实验定位排名变化的原因，分析边界表示尺度。

Result: 联合训练下Pre-norm和Post-norm几乎无差异（验证CE差0.0004）；课程增长下Post-norm比Pre-norm好0.0328，差异大一个数量级。排名在课程过程中交叉（Post-norm在添加块后领先）。Post-norm保持稳定的残差尺度，Pre-norm出现结构标记尺度漂移。

Conclusion: 归一化位置和训练课程在蒸馏设置中是耦合的设计选择，Post-norm在课程增长中更有利，因其能更好地处理边界条件。

Abstract: Pre-norm is the standard normalization placement in modern Transformers because it facilitates joint optimization of full-depth models. We ask whether this preference persists when depth is introduced through a curriculum. In curriculum depth growth, each appended block receives the boundary representation produced by a trained prefix, making normalization placement relevant to forward conditioning. We therefore test whether placement and training curriculum interact. In a controlled distillation study with a Qwen3-8B teacher and a nine-layer student, pre-norm and post-norm are indistinguishable under joint training, differing by $0.0004$ validation CE, while post-norm improves over pre-norm by $0.0328$ under curriculum growth, an order of magnitude larger. A post-joint control matched by student active-layer tokens remains worse than post-grow, which rules out compute as the sole explanation. The ranking crosses over during the curriculum: post-norm takes the lead once blocks are appended. Single-block and freeze controls localize the ranking change to block appending rather than shallow-block quality or retraining. Boundary diagnostics associate post-norm with stable residual scales and pre-norm with structural-token scale drift; on a fixed batch, the final pre-grow block is also nearly identity-mapped. Together with the phase-wise crossover, these observations are consistent with boundary-scale conditioning after new blocks are appended. The results motivate treating normalization placement and training curriculum as coupled design choices in this distillation setting.

</details>


### [226] [SkillShapley: Boundary-Adaptive Shapley Valuation for Skill Step Attribution in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13173)
*Chang Liu,Yuqi Zhang,Yiman Zhong,Boyi Liu,Hengjun Wang,Shuyue Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Proposes SkillShapley, a Shapley value-based attribution framework to quantify the contribution of individual steps in agent skills, enabling identification of high- or low-value steps through efficient coalition sampling.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing agent skills lack understanding of how each step contributes to overall performance; there is an open problem in quantifying individual step contributions within an agent skill.

Method: Models skill-step attribution as a Shapley value-based contribution estimation problem. SkillShapley operates in two phases: first identifies informative coalitional regions, then adaptively samples new coalitions that yield reusable marginal evidence, leveraging insights about discretized rewards and additive step interactions.

Result: Experiments on SkillsBench demonstrate that SkillShapley effectively and efficiently identifies high- or low-value skill steps, providing key takeaways for agent skill creation.

Conclusion: SkillShapley can effectively attribute step-level contributions in agent skills, offering practical insights for skill creation.

Abstract: Agent skills are crucial external instructions that enable language agents to execute long procedural tasks such as coding or document processing. Existing agent skills are primarily created through human manual crafting or agent execution traces, with limited understanding of how each step contributes to overall skill performance on specific tasks; i.e., there remains an open problem in quantifying the contribution of individual steps within an agent skill. To address this issue, we first model skill-step attribution as a Shapley value-based contribution estimation problem, and then propose SkillShapley, a step-level attribution framework for agent skills. Notably, SkillShapley operates in two phases, motivated by key empirical insights, i.e., discretized benchmark rewards that create sharp performance cliffs, and step interactions that are largely additive rather than synergistic. Specifically, it first identifies informative coalitional regions, and then adaptively samples new coalitions that can yield reusable marginal evidence. Experiments on skills from the widely adopted SkillsBench demonstrate that our SkillShapley can effectively and efficiently identify high- or low-value skill steps, providing several key takeaways for agent skill creation.

</details>


### [227] [Teach the Magnitude, Not the Direction: Verifier-Bounded Credit Assignment for Multi-Turn Multi-step LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13179)
*Zechuan Wang,Siyuan Lu,Hongxuan Zhang,Linjian Mo,Chenyi Zhuang,Leilei Gan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CrEST is a hierarchical credit assignment framework for multi-turn tool-use agents that combines RL's verifier-bounded performance ceiling with dense token-level supervision from a self-teacher. It uses turn-segmented verified advantages for inter-turn credit and entropy-gated self-teacher modulation for intra-turn token contributions, outperforming both RL and distillation baselines on BFCL V3 and WildToolBench.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing RLVR methods suffer from trajectory-level credit assignment conflating heterogeneous per-turn outcomes into a single reward signal, while on-policy distillation provides dense per-token supervision but is either teacher-bounded or prone to gradient concentration collapse. The goal is to retain RL's verifier-bounded ceiling while incorporating dense token-level signals.

Method: CrEST is a hierarchical credit assignment framework with two levels: (1) turn-segmented verified advantages to address inter-turn dilution, and (2) entropy-gated self-teacher modulation to refine intra-turn token contributions. The teacher's role is reduced from determining update directions to modulating update magnitudes.

Result: Experiments on BFCL V3 and WildToolBench show CrEST consistently outperforms both RL and distillation baselines across two model scales, with largest gains on long-trajectory and strict session-level metrics.

Conclusion: The teacher's role in policy optimization can be reduced from determining update directions to modulating update magnitudes, unlocking dense credit assignment without sacrificing the verifier-bounded ceiling.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) offers a verifier-bounded performance ceiling for training multi-turn tool-use agents, yet its trajectory-level credit assignment conflates heterogeneous per-turn outcomes into a single reward signal. On-policy distillation provides dense per-token supervision but is either teacher-bounded or prone to gradient concentration collapse. We introduce $\textbf{CrEST}$, a hierarchical credit assignment framework that retains RL's verifier-bounded ceiling while incorporating dense token-level signals from a privileged self-teacher. $\textbf{CrEST}$ resolves credit at two levels: turn-segmented verified advantages address inter-turn dilution, while entropy-gated self-teacher modulation refines intra-turn token contributions. Experiments on BFCL V3 and WildToolBench show that $\textbf{CrEST}$ consistently outperforms both RL and distillation baselines across two model scales, with the largest gains on long-trajectory and strict session-level metrics. Our work demonstrates that the teacher's role in policy optimization can be reduced from determining update directions to modulating update magnitudes, unlocking dense credit assignment without sacrificing the verifier-bounded ceiling.

</details>


### [228] [TsuGO: Probing Search Efficiency in LLM Reasoning via Go Life-and-Death Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13221)
*Shunwen Bai,Ziping Ma,Chaoyang Zhang,Yarong Wang,Jiale Liu,Zhen Qin,Qingpei Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TsuGO is a process-level reasoning benchmark for evaluating search efficiency in LLM reasoning through Go life-and-death problems, showing that current LLMs struggle with organized search and that longer CoT or higher token efficiency does not imply better search.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM reasoning evaluation methods fail to capture how models plan reasoning paths and allocate reasoning resources (search organization). Prior process-level methods focus on coherence/redundancy of CoT, and most benchmarks have single objectives solvable by static capabilities, leaving search organization unmeasured.

Method: The authors introduce TsuGO, a benchmark using Go life-and-death problems (tsumego) which provide closed, verifiable solution spaces with adversarial structure. It constrains solution space to disentangle domain knowledge from search organization, parses CoT into a structured search tree, and reports Search Efficiency, Token Efficiency, and other diagnostic metrics/visualizations.

Result: Current LLMs remain far from stable tsumego solving. Stronger models succeed by finding correct candidate earlier and sustaining effort on productive branches, but most models behave like unguided search algorithms rather than neural-guided KataGo. Longer CoT or higher Token Efficiency does not necessarily imply better search.

Conclusion: Search organization and reasoning-resource allocation are missing dimensions in LLM reasoning evaluation. The benchmark reveals that LLM reasoning lacks effective search organization, highlighting a gap that needs to be addressed for advancing LLM reasoning capabilities.

Abstract: The evaluation of LLM reasoning is moving from final-answer accuracy to process-level assessment, yet existing methods still fail to capture how models plan reasoning paths and allocate reasoning resources--that is, how they organize search. Prior process-level methods focus on the coherence and redundancy of chain-of-thought (CoT), and most benchmark tasks have a single objective solvable by static capabilities such as derivation and tool use, leaving search organization unmeasured. We introduce TsuGO, a process-level reasoning benchmark for evaluating Search Efficiency in LLM reasoning through Go life-and-death problems. These problems provide closed and verifiable solution spaces with an inherent adversarial structure, making candidate generation, response checking, branch comparison, and backtracking necessary parts of reasoning rather than incidental trace patterns. By constraining the solution space, TsuGO disentangles domain knowledge from search organization, parses CoT into a structured search tree, and reports Search Efficiency together with Token Efficiency and other diagnostic metrics and visualizations. Experiments show that current LLMs remain far from stable tsumego solving: stronger models succeed by finding the correct candidate earlier and sustaining effort on productive branches, but most models still behave much closer to unguided search algorithms than to neural-guided KataGo. Longer CoT or higher Token Efficiency does not necessarily imply better search. Our results identify search organization and reasoning-resource allocation as missing dimensions in LLM reasoning evaluation.

</details>


### [229] [Capability Sheaves for Compositional Agent-Harness Repair: Controlled Quotients and a Real-Repository Stress Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13228)
*Saveliy Batruin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用有限能力层（capability sheaf）对智能体系统中组件状态不一致导致的失败建模，通过精确约束满足问题（CSP）定义接受性，并利用线性化相对上同调类提供诊断和搜索功能。在受控实验中，通过商掉共边界（coboundaries）将候选预算从2000降到1000，且精确CSP与商集匹配，表明对陈旧代表的不变性。在真实世界补丁选择任务中，该方法未能显著优于非上同调选择器，发现门失败，无法确认实际优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统结合了检索、路由、状态、来源和验证等功能，但局部成功的组件可能在共享状态上产生分歧，导致失败。作者希望用能力层模型来捕捉这种不一致性，并提供诊断和修复机制。

Method: 使用有限能力层，茎（stalks）编码类型化行为签名，限制映射保留共享字段。通过精确CSP定义接受运行，线性化相对上同调类提供诊断和搜索。在受控实验中引入隐藏内部中介，通过商掉共边界减少候选预算。在SWE-bench Multilingual的补丁选择任务中测试，构建池级和候选索引级修复。

Result: 受控实验表明精确CSP与商集匹配，证明对陈旧代表的不变性。在真实任务中，候选索引级修复在848/875个候选上非平凡，在120/160个问题中变化。解决了118个问题，与非上同调选择器的116个无显著差异（p=0.75）。留一仓库排除的弃权门达到127/160，与强锚点持平，但超过其匹配门一个问题（p=1.0）。发现门失败，确认拆分保持密封。

Conclusion: 该研究支持受控不变性机制和可识别性校正，但未证明上同调方法在实际中具有优势。

Abstract: Agent harnesses combine retrieval, routing, state, provenance, and verification, but locally successful components may disagree on shared state. We model this failure with a finite \emph{capability sheaf}: stalks encode typed behavior signatures, restriction maps retain shared fields, and accepted runs are useful global sections. An exact finite constraint-satisfaction problem (CSP) defines acceptance, while a linearized relative cohomology class provides a diagnostic and search feature.
  A controlled experiment over 20 task clusters introduces hidden interior mediators whose raw states are nuisance variables. Quotienting their coboundaries reduces the candidate budget from 2,000 to 1,000 per cluster; aligning the hidden state removes the gap. Exact CSP matches the quotient, so the result demonstrates invariance to stale representatives, not superiority over exact reasoning.
  We then test the method on a discovery split from the SWE-bench Multilingual pool of PatchFuseBench: 160 issues from 20 repositories, 875 real candidate patches, 2,579 source-aware edit atoms, and 153 newly executed patches. A first pool-level construction is constant because $[b-Dx]=[b]$ in $\operatorname{coker}D$ and therefore cannot rank configurations. A candidate-indexed repair is nontrivial on 848/875 candidates and varies within 120/160 issues. It resolves 118 issues versus 116 for a matched noncohomological selector, but the difference is not supported across repositories (exact sign-flip $p=0.75$). A leave-one-repository-out abstention gate reaches 127/160, tying the strong anchor and exceeding its matched gate by one issue ($p=1.0$). The discovery gate therefore fails and the confirmatory split remains sealed. The study supports the controlled invariance mechanism and an identifiability correction, but not a real-world cohomological advantage.

</details>


### [230] [vToken: Token-Level Virtualization for Reclaimable KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13263)
*Yuanhang Gao,Xiangrui Yang,Yuanfeng Chen,Hongjia Chen,Qianru Lv,Wenfei Wu,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: vToken is a lightweight token-level virtualization layer for LLM serving that decouples logical token liveness from physical block placement to eliminate intra-block fragmentation in KV cache management, reducing retained KV blocks by 27.2%-72.3% and improving throughput by up to 1.37×.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large language model serving faces a critical memory bottleneck due to KV cache growth with sequence length and batch size. PagedAttention uses fixed-size blocks to reduce allocator-level fragmentation, but recent KV eviction algorithms operate at token granularity finer than block-level management, causing intra-block fragmentation that leaves a large fraction of allocated KV memory unreclaimable.

Method: vToken introduces a token-level virtualization layer that decouples logical token liveness from physical block placement. It maintains a stable logical token view through token-table indirection and realizes physical reclamation by repacking live tokens asynchronously. The design preserves PagedAttention kernels and CUDA Graph compatibility.

Result: Implemented in vLLM and evaluated with H2O, Random, and Scissorhands across models. Compared with a paired Naive-Evict baseline, vToken reduces retained KV blocks per request by 27.2%-72.3%, improves SLA-constrained throughput by up to 1.37×, extends maximum feasible concurrency by up to 2× under constrained active-KV budget, and reduces per-policy integration footprint from 500+ lines to under 50.

Conclusion: vToken effectively eliminates intra-block fragmentation in KV cache management with minimal integration overhead, providing substantial improvements in memory efficiency, throughput, and concurrency for LLM serving systems.

Abstract: Large language model serving faces a critical memory bottleneck: the KV cache grows with sequence length and batch size. PagedAttention uses fixed-size memory blocks to reduce allocator-level fragmentation, but recent KV eviction algorithms operate at a token granularity finer than block-level management. This mismatch causes intra-block fragmentation, leaving a large fraction of allocated KV memory unreclaimable. We present vToken, a lightweight token-level virtualization layer that decouples logical token liveness from physical block placement. vToken maintains a stable logical token view through token-table indirection and realizes physical reclamation by repacking live tokens asynchronously. The design preserves PagedAttention kernels and CUDA Graph compatibility. We implement vToken in vLLM and evaluate it with H2O, Random, and Scissorhands across models. Compared with a paired Naive-Evict baseline, vToken reduces retained KV blocks per request by 27.2\%--72.3\% and improves SLA-constrained throughput by up to 1.37$\times$. Under a constrained active-KV budget, it extends the maximum feasible concurrency by up to 2$\times$, while reducing the per-policy integration footprint from 500+ lines to under 50.

</details>


### [231] [Sovereign by necessity? Frontier AI export controls, cyber security, and the limits of national AI capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13272)
*Alan Woodward,Andrew Rogoyski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了前沿AI模型在少数国家集中、政府控制其出口对网络防御与主权的影响。文章指出前沿AI访问正成为国家网络防御的组成部分且可被撤销，并提出分层策略：协商访问保障、推理层主权、开放权重模型对冲、区域能力池化、人才培养和基础网络韧性投资。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026年美国要求领先AI开发商在向外国人（包括美国境内外国居民）发布最先进模型前需获得许可证，导致模型全球撤回；同时首个大规模AI自主网络间谍活动被记录，前沿AI正在改变网络攻击与防御的经济性。这两个发展相互作用，且市场动态异常，需要研究其对国家网络防御和主权的影响。

Method: 基于训练成本、算力集中度、国家AI项目支持等证据，分析小型、中型及大型国家实现AI主权的现实可行性，并评估开放权重模型的能力与政治风险。

Result: 前沿AI访问正成为国家网络防御的一部分，但这种访问可能被撤销；除少数国家外，主权能力建设仅部分可行。开放权重模型比通常认为的更有能力但也更具政治风险。近期风险更多在于强大模型的部署与管控方式，而非其表面性能。

Conclusion: 提出分层策略：协商访问保障、推理层主权、开放权重模型对冲、区域能力池化、持续人才培养、基础网络韧性投资。强调开放权重对冲比预期更有效但政治风险更大，近期风险在于模型如何部署和管控。

Abstract: A small number of firms based in two states produce the most capable frontier AI models. The governments of those states have shown both the legal power and the political will to decide which other countries may use these systems. In June 2026 the United States required a leading developer to obtain licences before releasing its most advanced models to any foreign person, including foreign nationals resident in the United States. The affected models were withdrawn worldwide at short notice, partly because the restriction proved impractical to administer. This followed within months of the first documented case of a largely autonomous, AI-run cyber espionage campaign, and coincided with mounting evidence that frontier models alter the economics of both cyber attack and cyber defence. This article examines how these two developments interact, and situates them within the unusual market dynamics now driving large-scale AI development. It argues that access to frontier AI is becoming part of national cyber defence, that such access can be revoked, and that the obvious remedy of sovereign capability remains only partly feasible for all but a handful of states. Drawing on evidence about training costs, the concentration of computing power and the support offered by national AI programmes, it asks what sovereignty can realistically mean for small and middle powers, and for large powers as well. The article proposes a layered strategy: negotiated access guarantees, sovereignty at the level of inference, hedging with open-weight models, pooled regional capability, sustained talent development and continued investment in basic cyber resilience. The open-weight hedge proves at once more capable and more politically exposed than is commonly assumed. Much of the near-term risk lies in how capable models are deployed and contained rather than in their apparent performance.

</details>


### [232] [Towards Context-Aware Clinical Motion Understanding in Daily Living at Home: Freezing of Gait Detection with Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13283)
*Vayalet Stefanova,Diwas Lamsal,Margot Genbrugge,Maxim Yudayev,Christian Schlenstedt,Moran Gilat,Bart Vanrumste,Benjamin Filtjens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过同步第一人称视频和可穿戴IMU传感器，在13名帕金森病患者的居家环境中收集数据，用于冻结步态（FOG）检测。结果表明，基于IMU的时间卷积网络（TCN）在事件检测上表现最佳（F1=42.3, AUROC=83.0），而预训练的自我视频特征（V-JEPA2）虽不及IMU但仍有高于偶然水平的判别能力，且定性分析显示自我视频可能捕捉到与IMU无关的FOG相关信息，因此支持使用预训练自我视频表示为基于可穿戴传感器的临床运动理解添加上下文信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常生活中的运动理解需要超越运动学的情境信息，因为相似的惯性模式可能反映有意停顿、物体交互或病理运动障碍。自我中心视觉提供了任务相关的情境信息，有助于消除歧义。本研究通过帕金森病中的冻结步态检测这一受情境因素强烈影响的症状来探索这一挑战。

Method: 使用同步的自我中心视频、可穿戴IMU和专家标注的FOG标签，从13名PD参与者的家中收集数据。评估了来自预训练自我视频和时间序列基础模型的冻结表示，以及从头训练的基于IMU的TCN，采用留一被试交叉验证。

Result: 基于IMU的TCN取得了最强的事件检测性能（F1=42.3, AUROC=83.0），而V-JEPA2自我视频特征的表现较低（F1=32.6, AUROC=77.2）。自我视频单独未超越IMU传感，但显示出高于偶然水平的判别能力。定性分析表明自我中心视觉可能捕捉到与IMU独立的FOG相关信息。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持使用预训练的自我视频表示为基于可穿戴传感器的临床运动理解添加上下文信息，有助于更准确地理解日常生活中的运动。

Abstract: Understanding motion in daily living requires context beyond kinematics, because similar inertial patterns during activities of daily living (ADLs) can reflect intentional stopping, object interaction, or pathological movement impairment. Egocentric vision provides task-related context that may help disambiguate these cases. We investigate this challenge through freezing of gait (FOG) detection in Parkinson's disease (PD), a symptom strongly influenced by contextual factors during ADLs.
